mobile/src/mobile.js nouveau — pont Android + synchro
// Coffre Android — remplace le pont Electron (window.coffre) par les modules Capacitor
// et ajoute la synchronisation avec le PC. Chargé avant app.js.
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const Cap = window.Capacitor || {};
  const P = Cap.Plugins || {};
  const FS = P.Filesystem;
  const DATA = 'DATA';
  const REPO = 'BlancheLaGoat/Coffre';
  const UA = `Coffre/${window.COFFRE_VERSION || '1'} ( https://github.com/${REPO} )`;
  const COVER_RE = /^[\w.-]+\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i;
  const Core = window.CoffreCore;

  window.CARD_IMG_BASE = 'cards/';

  // ---------- Fichiers ----------
  async function readText(path) {
    try { return (await FS.readFile({ path, directory: DATA, encoding: 'utf8' })).data; } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  async function writeText(path, text) {
    await FS.writeFile({ path, directory: DATA, encoding: 'utf8', data: text, recursive: true });
  }
  async function readJson(path, fallback) {
    const t = await readText(path);
    if (t == null) return fallback;
    try { return JSON.parse(t); } catch (e) { return fallback; }
  }
  async function writeB64(path, b64) {
    await FS.writeFile({ path, directory: DATA, data: b64, recursive: true });
  }
  async function readB64(path) {
    return (await FS.readFile({ path, directory: DATA })).data;
  }
  async function listCovers() {
    try {
      const r = await FS.readdir({ path: 'pochettes', directory: DATA });
      return (r.files || []).map(f => typeof f === 'string' ? f : f.name).filter(n => COVER_RE.test(n));
    } catch (e) { return []; }
  }
  const newCoverName = ext => 'p' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6) + '.' + (ext || 'jpg');

  // ---------- Réseau (en natif : pas de blocage CORS, User-Agent autorisé) ----------
  async function http(opts) {
    const o = { method: 'GET', headers: {}, connectTimeout: 15000, readTimeout: 30000, ...opts };
    if (P.CapacitorHttp && Cap.isNativePlatform && Cap.isNativePlatform()) {
      return P.CapacitorHttp.request(o);
    }
    // Hors téléphone (tests dans un navigateur)
    const ctl = new AbortController(); const t = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), o.readTimeout);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(o.url, { method: o.method, headers: o.headers, body: o.data == null ? undefined : (typeof o.data === 'string' ? o.data : JSON.stringify(o.data)), signal: ctl.signal });
      const ct = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      let data;
      if (o.responseType === 'blob') { const buf = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer()); let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i += 32768) s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, buf.subarray(i, i + 32768)); data = btoa(s); }
      else if (ct.includes('json')) data = await res.json();
      else data = await res.text();
      return { status: res.status, data, headers: { 'content-type': ct } };
    } finally { clearTimeout(t); }
  }
  const asJson = d => typeof d === 'string' ? JSON.parse(d) : d;

  async function getJson(url, headers) {
    const res = await http({ url, headers: { 'User-Agent': UA, Accept: 'application/json', ...(headers || {}) } });
    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) throw new Error('Discogs a refusé tes identifiants. Vérifie ta clé et ton secret (ou ton token) dans les réglages.');
    if (res.status === 429 || res.status === 503) throw new Error('Le site limite les recherches. Attends quelques secondes et réessaie.');
    if (res.status < 200 || res.status >= 300) throw new Error(`Le serveur a répondu ${res.status}.`);
    return asJson(res.data);
  }
  const vinylApi = Core.createVinylApi(getJson);

  // ---------- Fichier choisi par l'utilisateur ----------
  function pickFile(accept) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const inp = document.createElement('input');
      inp.type = 'file'; inp.accept = accept; inp.style.display = 'none';
      inp.addEventListener('change', () => { resolve(inp.files[0] || null); inp.remove(); });
      document.body.appendChild(inp); inp.click();
      // Si l'utilisateur annule, aucun événement : la promesse reste en attente, sans conséquence.
    });
  }
  function fileToImage(file) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file); const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => { resolve(img); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000); };
      img.onerror = reject; img.src = url;
    });
  }
  function imageToJpegB64(img, max) {
    const k = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight));
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * k); c.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * k);
    c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    return c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.88).split(',')[1];
  }

  // ---------- Données ----------
  const defaultData = () => ({ version: 1, fm: {}, vinyls: {}, settings: { source: 'musicbrainz', discogsToken: '', layout: 'gal' } });
  let coversUrl = '';
  let lastSyncedJson = '';

  // ---------- Mises à jour (APK publiée sur GitHub) ----------
  let updateCb = () => {};
  let apkUrl = '';
  const newer = (a, b) => {
    const pa = String(a).replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number), pb = String(b).replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { if ((pa[i] || 0) !== (pb[i] || 0)) return (pa[i] || 0) > (pb[i] || 0); }
    return false;
  };
  async function checkUpdate() {
    try {
      const res = await http({ url: `https://api.github.com/repos/${REPO}/releases/latest`, headers: { 'User-Agent': UA, Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json' } });
      if (res.status === 404) return { status: 'none' };
      if (res.status < 200 || res.status >= 300) throw new Error(`GitHub a répondu ${res.status}.`);
      const r = asJson(res.data);
      const apk = (r.assets || []).find(a => /\.apk$/i.test(a.name));
      if (apk && newer(r.tag_name, window.COFFRE_VERSION)) {
        apkUrl = apk.browser_download_url;
        const s = { status: 'ready', version: String(r.tag_name).replace(/^v/, '') };
        updateCb(s); return s;
      }
      return { status: 'none' };
    } catch (e) {
      return { status: 'error', message: e.message || String(e) };
    }
  }

  window.coffre = {
    async info() {
      try {
        const u = await FS.getUri({ path: 'pochettes', directory: DATA });
        coversUrl = Cap.convertFileSrc(u.uri) + '/';
      } catch (e) { coversUrl = ''; }
      return { version: window.COFFRE_VERSION || '', coversUrl, platform: 'mobile' };
    },
    async load() {
      try { await FS.mkdir({ path: 'pochettes', directory: DATA, recursive: true }); } catch (e) { /* existe déjà */ }
      const d = await readJson('coffre.json', null) || await readJson('coffre.precedent.json', null) || defaultData();
      d.fm = d.fm || {}; d.vinyls = d.vinyls || {}; d.settings = { ...defaultData().settings, ...(d.settings || {}) };
      return d;
    },
    async save(d) {
      const text = JSON.stringify(d);
      const prev = await readText('coffre.json');
      if (prev) await writeText('coffre.precedent.json', prev);
      await writeText('coffre.json', text);
      if (JSON.stringify({ fm: d.fm, vinyls: d.vinyls }) !== lastSyncedJson) Sync.soon();
      return true;
    },
    search: args => vinylApi.search(args),
    details: args => vinylApi.details(args),
    async downloadCover(url) {
      if (!url) return '';
      try {
        const res = await http({ url, responseType: 'blob', headers: { 'User-Agent': UA } });
        if (res.status < 200 || res.status >= 300 || !res.data) return '';
        const ct = (res.headers && (res.headers['content-type'] || res.headers['Content-Type'])) || '';
        const ext = /png/.test(ct) ? 'png' : /webp/.test(ct) ? 'webp' : 'jpg';
        const name = newCoverName(ext);
        await writeB64('pochettes/' + name, res.data);
        return name;
      } catch (e) { return ''; }
    },
    async pickCover() {
      const f = await pickFile('image/*'); if (!f) return '';
      try {
        const b64 = imageToJpegB64(await fileToImage(f), 800);
        const name = newCoverName('jpg');
        await writeB64('pochettes/' + name, b64);
        return name;
      } catch (e) { return ''; }
    },
    async deleteCover(name) {
      if (!COVER_RE.test(name || '')) return false;
      try { await FS.deleteFile({ path: 'pochettes/' + name, directory: DATA }); } catch (e) { /* déjà absente */ }
      return true;
    },
    async exportBackup(d) {
      const covers = {};
      for (const v of Object.values(d.vinyls || {})) {
        if (v && !v.deleted && v.cover && COVER_RE.test(v.cover)) { try { covers[v.cover] = await readB64('pochettes/' + v.cover); } catch (e) { /* manquante */ } }
      }
      const clean = { ...d, settings: { ...d.settings, discogsSecret: '', discogsToken: '' } };
      const name = `Coffre-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.coffre`;
      await FS.writeFile({ path: name, directory: 'DOCUMENTS', encoding: 'utf8', data: JSON.stringify({ format: 'coffre-backup', version: 1, data: clean, covers }), recursive: true });
      return { ok: true, path: 'Documents/' + name };
    },
    async importBackup() {
      const f = await pickFile('.coffre,.json,application/json,application/octet-stream,*/*'); if (!f) return { ok: false };
      const b = JSON.parse(await f.text());
      const data = b.format === 'coffre-backup' ? b.data : b;
      if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || (!data.fm && !data.vinyls)) throw new Error("Ce fichier n'est pas une sauvegarde Coffre.");
      for (const [name, b64] of Object.entries(b.covers || {})) if (COVER_RE.test(name)) await writeB64('pochettes/' + name, b64);
      const now = Date.now();
      const restamp = m => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(m || {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, { ...v, mt: now }]));
      const cur = window.CoffreApp.data;
      const merged = Core.mergeCollections(cur, { fm: restamp(data.fm), vinyls: restamp(data.vinyls) });
      const out = { ...cur, ...merged };
      await window.coffre.save(out);
      return { ok: true, data: out };
    },
    openExternal(url) {
      if (!/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) return;
      if (P.Browser) return P.Browser.open({ url });
      window.open(url, '_blank');
    },
    openDataDir() {},
    checkUpdate,
    installUpdate() { if (apkUrl) return window.coffre.openExternal(apkUrl); },
    onUpdate(cb) { updateCb = cb; }
  };

  // ============================================================
  //  SYNCHRONISATION AVEC LE PC
  // ============================================================
  const Sync = {
    pair: null,        // { token, port, hosts: [], host, pcName, lastSync, lastError }
    busy: false,
    timer: null,
    status: '',

    async init() {
      this.pair = await readJson('appairage.json', null);
      const app = window.CoffreApp.data;
      lastSyncedJson = this.pair ? '' : JSON.stringify({ fm: app.fm, vinyls: app.vinyls });
      if (P.App) P.App.addListener('resume', () => this.run());
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (!document.hidden) this.run(); });
      setInterval(() => { if (!document.hidden) this.run(); }, 45000);
      this.run();
    },
    async savePair() { await writeText('appairage.json', JSON.stringify(this.pair)); },
    soon() { clearTimeout(this.timer); if (this.pair) this.timer = setTimeout(() => this.run(), 2500); },

    async call(host, path, opts) {
      const res = await http({
        url: `http://${host}:${this.pair.port}${path}`,
        connectTimeout: 4000, readTimeout: 20000,
        ...opts,
        headers: { 'X-Coffre-Token': this.pair.token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(opts && opts.headers || {}) }
      });
      if (res.status === 401) { const e = new Error('Le PC a refusé le code. Associe à nouveau le téléphone (Réglages → Associer au PC).'); e.auth = true; throw e; }
      if (res.status < 200 || res.status >= 300) throw new Error(`Le PC a répondu ${res.status}.`);
      return asJson(res.data);
    },

    // Essaie la dernière adresse qui a marché, puis les autres.
    async reach(fn) {
      const hosts = [...new Set([this.pair.host, ...(this.pair.hosts || [])].filter(Boolean))];
      let last = null;
      for (const h of hosts) {
        try { const r = await fn(h); this.pair.host = h; return r; }
        catch (e) { if (e.auth) throw e; last = e; }
      }
      const e = new Error("PC injoignable. Vérifie que Coffre est ouvert sur le PC et que le téléphone est sur le même Wi-Fi.");
      e.cause = last; throw e;
    },

    async run(manual) {
      if (!this.pair || this.busy) return false;
      this.busy = true; this.setStatus('Synchronisation…');
      const App = window.CoffreApp;
      try {
        const d = App.data;
        const sent = { fm: d.fm, vinyls: d.vinyls };
        const res = await this.reach(h => this.call(h, '/api/sync', { method: 'POST', data: { device: 'Téléphone', ...sent } }));
        // Fusion avec ce qui a pu changer sur le téléphone pendant l'échange
        const merged = Core.mergeCollections({ fm: App.data.fm, vinyls: App.data.vinyls }, res);
        if (!Core.sameCollections(merged, App.data)) {
          lastSyncedJson = JSON.stringify(merged);
          App.applyRemote(merged);
          App.scheduleSave();
        }
        lastSyncedJson = JSON.stringify({ fm: merged.fm, vinyls: merged.vinyls });
        await this.syncCovers(merged, res.covers || []);
        this.pair.pcName = res.name || this.pair.pcName;
        this.pair.lastSync = Date.now(); this.pair.lastError = '';
        await this.savePair();
        this.setStatus('');
        if (manual) App.toast('Synchronisé avec le PC');
        // Un changement fait pendant l'échange doit repartir vers le PC
        if (JSON.stringify({ fm: App.data.fm, vinyls: App.data.vinyls }) !== lastSyncedJson) this.soon();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        this.pair.lastError = e.message || String(e);
        await this.savePair();
        this.setStatus('');
        if (manual) App.toast(this.pair.lastError);
        return false;
      } finally {
        this.busy = false;
        this.refreshSettings();
      }
    },

    async syncCovers(col, pcCovers) {
      const used = new Set(Object.values(col.vinyls || {}).filter(v => v && !v.deleted && v.cover && COVER_RE.test(v.cover)).map(v => v.cover));
      const mine = new Set(await listCovers());
      const theirs = new Set(pcCovers);
      let got = 0;
      for (const name of used) {
        try {
          if (!mine.has(name) && theirs.has(name)) {
            const r = await this.call(this.pair.host, `/api/cover?b64=1&name=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { method: 'GET' });
            if (r.data) { await writeB64('pochettes/' + name, r.data); got++; }
          } else if (mine.has(name) && !theirs.has(name)) {
            await this.call(this.pair.host, `/api/cover?name=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { method: 'PUT', data: { data: await readB64('pochettes/' + name) } });
          }
        } catch (e) { if (e.auth) throw e; /* on réessaiera à la prochaine synchro */ }
      }
      if (got) window.CoffreApp.renderVinyls();
    },

    // Contenu du QR code affiché par le PC : {t:'coffre', v:1, k:token, p:port, h:[ips]}
    async pairWith(text) {
      let q;
      try { q = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { q = null; }
      if (!q || q.t !== 'coffre' || !q.k || !Array.isArray(q.h)) throw new Error("Ce QR code ne vient pas de Coffre. Ouvre Réglages sur le PC et scanne le code de la section Téléphone.");
      const prev = this.pair;
      this.pair = { token: q.k, port: q.p || 47800, hosts: q.h, host: '', pcName: '', lastSync: 0, lastError: '' };
      try {
        const ping = await this.reach(h => this.call(h, '/api/ping', { method: 'GET' }));
        this.pair.pcName = ping.name || 'PC';
        await this.savePair();
        lastSyncedJson = '';
        await this.run();
        return this.pair.pcName;
      } catch (e) {
        this.pair = prev;
        throw e;
      }
    },

    async unpair() {
      this.pair = null;
      try { await FS.deleteFile({ path: 'appairage.json', directory: DATA }); } catch (e) { /* rien */ }
      this.refreshSettings();
    },

    setStatus(t) {
      this.status = t;
      const el = document.getElementById('savedState');
      if (el && t) el.textContent = t;
      else if (el && el.textContent === 'Synchronisation…') el.textContent = '';
      this.refreshSettings();
    },

    refreshSettings() {
      const box = document.getElementById('pcBox');
      if (box) box.innerHTML = pcSectionInner();
    }
  };

  const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  const fmtDate = ms => new Date(ms).toLocaleString('fr-FR', { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' });

  function pcSectionInner() {
    const p = Sync.pair;
    if (!p) {
      return `<p>Associe ton téléphone au PC pour que tout ce que tu ajoutes ici arrive aussi sur l'ordinateur, et inversement.</p>
        <p class="hint">Sur le PC : ouvre Coffre → ⚙ Réglages → section Téléphone. Puis touche le bouton ci-dessous et scanne le QR code.</p>
        <div class="actions" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="primary" data-act="pcPair">Associer au PC</button></div>`;
    }
    return `<dl class="kv"><dt>Ordinateur</dt><dd>${esc(p.pcName || 'PC')} (${esc(p.host || (p.hosts || [])[0] || '')})</dd>
        <dt>Dernière synchro</dt><dd>${Sync.busy ? 'en cours…' : p.lastSync ? fmtDate(p.lastSync) : 'jamais'}</dd></dl>
      ${p.lastError ? `<p class="error">${esc(p.lastError)}</p>` : ''}
      <div class="actions" style="justify-content:flex-start">
        <button class="primary" data-act="pcSync" ${Sync.busy ? 'disabled' : ''}>Synchroniser maintenant</button>
        <button data-act="pcPair">Associer à nouveau</button>
        <button class="danger" data-act="pcUnpair">Dissocier</button>
      </div>
      <p class="hint">La synchro se fait toute seule à l'ouverture de l'appli, après chaque ajout et toutes les 45 secondes, quand le PC est allumé avec Coffre ouvert sur le même Wi-Fi.</p>`;
  }

  window.CoffreExt = {
    settingsHtml() { return `<h3>Ordinateur</h3><div id="pcBox">${pcSectionInner()}</div>`; },
    async settingsClick(act) {
      if (act === 'pcSync') return Sync.run(true);
      if (act === 'pcUnpair') {
        if (!window.confirm('Dissocier ce téléphone du PC ? Ta collection reste sur les deux appareils.')) return;
        return Sync.unpair();
      }
      if (act === 'pcPair') {
        if (!window.CoffreScan) return;
        window.CoffreApp.closeModal();
        const text = await window.CoffreScan.scanQr();
        if (!text) return;
        window.CoffreApp.toast('Connexion au PC…');
        try {
          const name = await Sync.pairWith(text);
          window.CoffreApp.toast(`Associé à ${name}`);
        } catch (e) {
          window.CoffreApp.toast(e.message || String(e));
        }
      }
    },
    ready() {
      Sync.init();
      setTimeout(() => checkUpdate(), 4000);
      // Bouton retour d'Android : ferme la fenêtre ouverte, sinon quitte
      if (P.App) P.App.addListener('backButton', () => {
        if (window.CoffreScan && window.CoffreScan.isOpen()) return window.CoffreScan.close();
        const m = document.getElementById('modal');
        if (m && !m.hidden) return window.CoffreApp.closeModal();
        P.App.exitApp();
      });
    }
  };

  window.CoffreSync = Sync;
})();
mobile/src/scan.js nouveau — appareil photo, reconnaissance, codes-barres
// Coffre Android — appareil photo : reconnaissance des cartes FM par l'illustration,
// lecture du code-barres des vinyles et du QR code d'appairage. Chargé après app.js.
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const App = window.CoffreApp;
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const esc = App.esc;

  // ============================================================
  //  RECONNAISSANCE DES CARTES
  //  On regarde seulement l'illustration (pas le texte ni le code),
  //  donc une vieille carte japonaise est reconnue comme une carte récente.
  // ============================================================
  const ART = [0.12, 0.185, 0.88, 0.70];   // zone de l'illustration dans la carte (x0, y0, x1, y1)
  const CARD_RATIO = 421 / 614;            // largeur / hauteur d'une carte
  const G = 12, DIM = G * G * 3;
  const DESC_VERSION = 'v1';

  const cA = document.createElement('canvas'); cA.width = cA.height = 192;
  const cB = document.createElement('canvas'); cB.width = cB.height = 48;
  const xA = cA.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  const xB = cB.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  xA.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'; xB.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high';

  const lin = c => { c /= 255; return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : Math.pow((c + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4); };
  const LIN = new Float32Array(256); for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) LIN[i] = lin(i);
  const f = t => t > 0.008856 ? Math.cbrt(t) : 7.787 * t + 16 / 116;

  // Empreinte d'une zone d'image : grille 12×12 en couleurs LAB, normalisée
  // (insensible à la luminosité et à la balance des blancs).
  function describe(src, sx, sy, sw, sh) {
    xA.clearRect(0, 0, 192, 192);
    xA.drawImage(src, sx, sy, sw, sh, 0, 0, 192, 192);
    xB.drawImage(cA, 0, 0, 48, 48);
    const px = xB.getImageData(0, 0, 48, 48).data;
    const L = new Float32Array(G * G), A = new Float32Array(G * G), B = new Float32Array(G * G);
    for (let gy = 0; gy < G; gy++) for (let gx = 0; gx < G; gx++) {
      let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
      for (let y = 0; y < 4; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 4; x++) {
        const i = ((gy * 4 + y) * 48 + gx * 4 + x) * 4;
        r += px[i]; g += px[i + 1]; b += px[i + 2];
      }
      const R = LIN[Math.round(r / 16)], Gc = LIN[Math.round(g / 16)], Bc = LIN[Math.round(b / 16)];
      const X = (0.4124 * R + 0.3576 * Gc + 0.1805 * Bc) / 0.95047;
      const Y = 0.2126 * R + 0.7152 * Gc + 0.0722 * Bc;
      const Z = (0.0193 * R + 0.1192 * Gc + 0.9505 * Bc) / 1.08883;
      const fx = f(X), fy = f(Y), fz = f(Z);
      const k = gy * G + gx;
      L[k] = 116 * fy - 16; A[k] = 500 * (fx - fy); B[k] = 200 * (fy - fz);
    }
    const out = new Float32Array(DIM);
    [[L, 0.6], [A, 1], [B, 1]].forEach(([ch, w], c) => {
      let m = 0; for (const v of ch) m += v; m /= ch.length;
      let s = 0; for (const v of ch) s += (v - m) * (v - m); s = Math.sqrt(s / ch.length) + 1e-3;
      for (let i = 0; i < ch.length; i++) out[c * G * G + i] = (ch[i] - m) / s * w;
    });
    let n = 0; for (const v of out) n += v * v; n = Math.sqrt(n) || 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < DIM; i++) out[i] /= n;
    return out;
  }

  // Empreinte de l'illustration d'une carte occupant le rectangle (x, y, w, h) de l'image source
  function describeCard(src, x, y, w, h) {
    return describe(src, x + ART[0] * w, y + ART[1] * h, (ART[2] - ART[0]) * w, (ART[3] - ART[1]) * h);
  }

  // ---------- Empreintes de référence (calculées une fois sur le téléphone) ----------
  const REF = { ids: [], mat: null, ready: null };
  function loadImg(url) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => resolve(i); i.onerror = reject; i.src = url; });
  }
  function b64ToF32(b64) { const s = atob(b64); const u = new Uint8Array(s.length); for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u[i] = s.charCodeAt(i); return new Float32Array(u.buffer); }
  function f32ToB64(a) { const u = new Uint8Array(a.buffer); let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < u.length; i += 32768) s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, u.subarray(i, i + 32768)); return btoa(s); }

  function prepareRefs(onProgress) {
    if (REF.ready) return REF.ready;
    REF.ready = (async () => {
      const ids = [...App.HAS_IMG].sort();
      const key = `empreintes-${DESC_VERSION}-${ids.length}.bin`;
      const FS = window.Capacitor && window.Capacitor.Plugins && window.Capacitor.Plugins.Filesystem;
      try {
        const c = FS ? (await FS.readFile({ path: key, directory: 'DATA' })).data : null;
        if (c) { const m = b64ToF32(c); if (m.length === ids.length * DIM) { REF.ids = ids; REF.mat = m; return; } }
      } catch (e) { /* pas encore calculées */ }
      const mat = new Float32Array(ids.length * DIM);
      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        try {
          const img = await loadImg(App.cardImg(ids[i]));
          mat.set(describeCard(img, 0, 0, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight), i * DIM);
        } catch (e) { /* image manquante : empreinte nulle, jamais proposée */ }
        if (onProgress && i % 25 === 0) { onProgress(i / ids.length); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 0)); }
      }
      REF.ids = ids; REF.mat = mat;
      try { if (FS) await FS.writeFile({ path: key, directory: 'DATA', data: f32ToB64(mat) }); } catch (e) { /* on recalculera la prochaine fois */ }
    })();
    return REF.ready;
  }

  // Compare plusieurs cadrages légèrement décalés et garde, pour chaque carte, le meilleur score.
  const CROPS = [[0, 0, 1], [0, 0, 0.95], [0, 0, 1.05], [-0.03, 0, 1], [0.03, 0, 1], [0, -0.025, 1], [0, 0.025, 1]];
  function scoreRect(src, x, y, w, h) {
    const n = REF.ids.length, mat = REF.mat;
    const best = new Float32Array(n).fill(-2);
    for (const [dx, dy, s] of CROPS) {
      const cw = w * s, ch = h * s;
      const q = describeCard(src, x + (w - cw) / 2 + dx * w, y + (h - ch) / 2 + dy * h, cw, ch);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        let d = 0; const o = i * DIM;
        for (let j = 0; j < DIM; j++) d += mat[o + j] * q[j];
        if (d > best[i]) best[i] = d;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }
  function topK(scores, k) {
    const idx = Array.from(scores.keys()).sort((a, b) => scores[b] - scores[a]).slice(0, k);
    return idx.map(i => ({ n: REF.ids[i], score: scores[i] }));
  }

  // ============================================================
  //  APPAREIL PHOTO
  // ============================================================
  let stream = null, loopTimer = null, mode = '', qrResolve = null;
  const cam = { smooth: null, lead: '', leadCount: 0, armed: true, pauseUntil: 0, added: [] };
  // Réglages de l'ajout automatique (score de ressemblance entre -1 et 1)
  const AUTO = { score: 0.68, margin: 0.15, frames: 4, rearm: 0.55, rearmFrames: 3 };

  function buildOverlay() {
    if ($('scanView')) return;
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'scanView'; el.className = 'scan'; el.hidden = true;
    el.innerHTML = `
      <video id="scanVideo" playsinline muted autoplay></video>
      <div class="scan-frame" id="scanFrame"><i></i></div>
      <div class="scan-top">
        <button class="scan-close" data-scan="close" aria-label="Fermer">✕</button>
        <div class="scan-title" id="scanTitle"></div>
        <label class="scan-auto" id="scanAutoWrap"><input type="checkbox" id="scanAuto"> Ajout auto</label>
      </div>
      <div class="scan-bottom">
        <p class="scan-msg" id="scanMsg"></p>
        <div class="scan-cands" id="scanCands"></div>
        <div class="scan-actions" id="scanActions"></div>
      </div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    el.addEventListener('click', onClick);
    const cands = el.querySelector('#scanCands');
    const hold = () => { cam.pauseUntil = Date.now() + 1500; };
    cands.addEventListener('touchstart', hold, { passive: true });
    cands.addEventListener('pointerdown', hold);
    el.querySelector('#scanAuto').addEventListener('change', e => {
      App.data.settings.scanAuto = e.target.checked; App.scheduleSave();
      cam.armed = true; cam.leadCount = 0;
    });
  }

  async function startCamera() {
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) throw new Error("L'appareil photo n'est pas disponible.");
    stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      audio: false,
      video: { facingMode: { ideal: 'environment' }, width: { ideal: 1920 }, height: { ideal: 1080 } }
    });
    const v = $('scanVideo');
    v.srcObject = stream;
    await v.play().catch(() => {});
    await new Promise(r => { if (v.videoWidth) r(); else v.onloadedmetadata = () => r(); });
    // Mise au point continue si le téléphone la propose
    try {
      const track = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
      const caps = track.getCapabilities ? track.getCapabilities() : {};
      if (caps.focusMode && caps.focusMode.includes('continuous')) await track.applyConstraints({ advanced: [{ focusMode: 'continuous' }] });
    } catch (e) { /* pas grave */ }
  }

  function stopCamera() {
    clearTimeout(loopTimer); loopTimer = null;
    if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
    stream = null;
    const v = $('scanVideo'); if (v) v.srcObject = null;
  }

  async function open(m) {
    buildOverlay();
    mode = m;
    const view = $('scanView');
    view.dataset.mode = m;
    view.hidden = false;
    $('scanCands').innerHTML = ''; $('scanActions').innerHTML = '';
    $('scanAutoWrap').hidden = m !== 'card';
    $('scanAuto').checked = App.data.settings.scanAuto !== false;
    $('scanTitle').textContent = m === 'card' ? 'Scanner des cartes' : m === 'vinyl' ? 'Code-barres du vinyle' : 'QR code du PC';
    cam.smooth = null; cam.lead = ''; cam.leadCount = 0; cam.armed = true; cam.lastAuto = ''; cam.added = [];
    msg(m === 'card' ? 'Place la carte dans le cadre, bien à plat.' : m === 'vinyl' ? "Vise le code-barres au dos de la pochette." : 'Vise le QR code affiché dans les réglages de Coffre sur le PC.');
    layoutFrame();
    try {
      if (m === 'card') {
        await prepareRefs(p => msg(`Préparation de la reconnaissance… ${Math.round(p * 100)} %`));
        if (mode !== m || view.hidden) return;
        msg('Place la carte dans le cadre, bien à plat.');
      }
      await startCamera();
      if (view.hidden) return stopCamera();
      layoutFrame();
      loop();
    } catch (e) {
      msg(e && e.name === 'NotAllowedError'
        ? "Coffre n'a pas accès à l'appareil photo. Autorise-le dans les paramètres Android (Applis → Coffre → Autorisations)."
        : "Impossible d'ouvrir l'appareil photo : " + (e.message || e));
    }
  }

  function close() {
    stopCamera();
    const view = $('scanView'); if (view) view.hidden = true;
    if (qrResolve) { qrResolve(''); qrResolve = null; }
    if (mode === 'card' && cam.added.length) App.toast(`${cam.added.length} carte${cam.added.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} ajoutée${cam.added.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
    mode = '';
  }

  const msg = t => { const m = $('scanMsg'); if (m) m.textContent = t; };

  // Cadre de visée : carte (portrait) ou bande large (code-barres / QR)
  function layoutFrame() {
    const fr = $('scanFrame'); if (!fr) return;
    const W = window.innerWidth, H = window.innerHeight;
    let w, h;
    if (mode === 'card') {
      const maxH = H * 0.56, maxW = W * 0.78;
      h = Math.min(maxH, maxW / CARD_RATIO); w = h * CARD_RATIO;
    } else if (mode === 'vinyl') { w = W * 0.86; h = Math.min(H * 0.22, w * 0.5); }
    else { w = Math.min(W * 0.7, H * 0.45); h = w; }
    fr.style.width = w + 'px'; fr.style.height = h + 'px';
    fr.style.left = (W - w) / 2 + 'px'; fr.style.top = Math.max(64, H * 0.40 - h / 2) + 'px';
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', layoutFrame);

  // Rectangle du cadre converti en pixels de la vidéo (la vidéo remplit l'écran en « cover »)
  function frameInVideo() {
    const v = $('scanVideo'), fr = $('scanFrame');
    const vw = v.videoWidth, vh = v.videoHeight;
    if (!vw || !vh) return null;
    const vr = v.getBoundingClientRect(), r = fr.getBoundingClientRect();
    const s = Math.max(vr.width / vw, vr.height / vh);
    const ox = vr.left + (vr.width - vw * s) / 2, oy = vr.top + (vr.height - vh * s) / 2;
    return { x: (r.left - ox) / s, y: (r.top - oy) / s, w: r.width / s, h: r.height / s, vw, vh };
  }

  function loop() {
    clearTimeout(loopTimer);
    if (!stream || !mode) return;
    const t0 = performance.now();
    try {
      if (mode === 'card') tickCard();
      else tickCode();
    } catch (e) { /* image pas prête */ }
    if (!stream) return;
    const spent = performance.now() - t0;
    loopTimer = setTimeout(loop, Math.max(120, (mode === 'card' ? 380 : 220) - spent));
  }

  // ---------- Cartes ----------
  function tickCard() {
    const r = frameInVideo(); if (!r) return;
    const raw = scoreRect($('scanVideo'), r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);
    // Lissage sur quelques images pour éviter que les propositions sautent
    if (!cam.smooth) cam.smooth = raw;
    else for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) cam.smooth[i] = cam.smooth[i] * 0.45 + raw[i] * 0.55;
    const top = topK(cam.smooth, 6);
    if (Date.now() >= cam.pauseUntil) renderCands(top);

    // Ajout automatique : la même carte en tête plusieurs fois de suite, nettement devant la 2e
    const [a, b] = top;
    const sure = a.score >= AUTO.score && a.score - b.score >= AUTO.margin;
    if (a.n === cam.lead && sure) cam.leadCount++; else { cam.lead = a.n; cam.leadCount = sure ? 1 : 0; }
    cam.lowCount = a.score < AUTO.rearm ? (cam.lowCount || 0) + 1 : 0;
    if (cam.lowCount >= AUTO.rearmFrames) cam.armed = true;   // carte retirée du cadre
    if (cam.armed === false && cam.lastAuto && a.n !== cam.lastAuto && sure && cam.leadCount >= AUTO.frames) cam.armed = true; // autre carte
    if ($('scanAuto').checked && cam.armed && cam.leadCount >= AUTO.frames) {
      cam.armed = false; cam.lastAuto = a.n;
      addCard(a.n, true);
    }
  }

  function renderCands(top) {
    const box = $('scanCands');
    box.innerHTML = top.map((c, i) => {
      const card = App.CARDS[Number(c.n) - 1];
      return `<button class="cand${i === 0 ? ' first' : ''}" data-scan="add" data-n="${c.n}">
        <img src="${App.cardImg(c.n)}" alt="">
        <span class="nm">${esc(card.name)}</span>
        <span class="sc">${Math.max(0, Math.round(c.score * 100))} %</span></button>`;
    }).join('');
    const own = n => (App.data.fm[n] && App.data.fm[n].qty) || 0;
    $('scanActions').innerHTML = cam.added.length
      ? `<span>Ajoutées : <b>${cam.added.length}</b> · dernière : ${esc(App.CARDS[Number(cam.added[cam.added.length - 1]) - 1].name)} (×${own(cam.added[cam.added.length - 1])})</span><button data-scan="undo">Annuler</button>`
      : `<span>Touche une carte pour l'ajouter${$('scanAuto').checked ? ', ou laisse-la dans le cadre pour l\'ajout auto' : ''}.</span>`;
  }

  function addCard(n, auto) {
    const q = App.changeQty(n, +1);
    cam.added.push(n);
    App.renderFm();
    if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(auto ? [30, 40, 30] : 30);
    const card = App.CARDS[Number(n) - 1];
    App.toast(`${card.name} ajoutée (×${q})`);
    const fr = $('scanFrame'); fr.classList.remove('ok'); void fr.offsetWidth; fr.classList.add('ok');
    cam.pauseUntil = 0;
    if (cam.smooth) renderCands(topK(cam.smooth, 6));
  }

  // ---------- Codes-barres et QR codes ----------
  const cC = document.createElement('canvas');
  const xC = cC.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  let detector = null, zx = null, busyDetect = false;

  function getDetector() {
    const formats = mode === 'qr' ? ['qr_code'] : ['ean_13', 'ean_8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e'];
    try {
      if ('BarcodeDetector' in window) return { kind: 'native', mode, d: new window.BarcodeDetector({ formats }) };
    } catch (e) { /* non disponible */ }
    return null;
  }
  function getZxing() {
    const Z = window.ZXing;
    const hints = new Map();
    hints.set(Z.DecodeHintType.POSSIBLE_FORMATS, mode === 'qr' ? [Z.BarcodeFormat.QR_CODE] : [Z.BarcodeFormat.EAN_13, Z.BarcodeFormat.EAN_8, Z.BarcodeFormat.UPC_A, Z.BarcodeFormat.UPC_E]);
    hints.set(Z.DecodeHintType.TRY_HARDER, true);
    const r = new Z.MultiFormatReader(); r.setHints(hints);
    return { mode, r };
  }

  function tickCode() {
    if (busyDetect) return;
    const v = $('scanVideo'), r = frameInVideo(); if (!r) return;
    // Zone visée un peu élargie, à la résolution de la vidéo
    const pad = 0.25;
    const x = Math.max(0, r.x - r.w * pad), y = Math.max(0, r.y - r.h * pad);
    const w = Math.min(r.vw - x, r.w * (1 + 2 * pad)), h = Math.min(r.vh - y, r.h * (1 + 2 * pad));
    const k = Math.min(1, 1280 / w);
    cC.width = Math.round(w * k); cC.height = Math.round(h * k);
    xC.drawImage(v, x, y, w, h, 0, 0, cC.width, cC.height);
    if (!detector || detector.mode !== mode) detector = getDetector() || { kind: 'none', mode };
    if (detector.kind === 'native') {
      busyDetect = true;
      detector.d.detect(cC).then(list => { busyDetect = false; if (list && list[0]) found(list[0].rawValue); else tryZxing(); })
        .catch(() => { busyDetect = false; detector = { kind: 'none', mode }; });
      return;
    }
    tryZxing();
  }
  function tryZxing() {
    if (!window.ZXing || !mode) return;
    if (!zx || zx.mode !== mode) zx = getZxing();
    try {
      const Z = window.ZXing;
      const bmp = new Z.BinaryBitmap(new Z.HybridBinarizer(new Z.HTMLCanvasElementLuminanceSource(cC)));
      const res = zx.r.decode(bmp);
      if (res) found(res.getText());
    } catch (e) { /* rien trouvé sur cette image */ }
  }

  function found(text) {
    if (!text || !mode) return;
    const m = mode;
    if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(40);
    if (m === 'qr') {
      const res = qrResolve; qrResolve = null;
      close();
      if (res) res(text);
      return;
    }
    if (m === 'vinyl') {
      const code = String(text).replace(/\D/g, '');
      close();
      App.showTab('vin');
      App.searchFor(code);
    }
  }

  // ---------- Boutons ----------
  function onClick(e) {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-scan]'); if (!b) return;
    const act = b.dataset.scan;
    if (act === 'close') return close();
    if (act === 'add') return addCard(b.dataset.n, false);
    if (act === 'undo') {
      const n = cam.added.pop(); if (!n) return;
      App.changeQty(n, -1); App.renderFm();
      App.toast(`${App.CARDS[Number(n) - 1].name} retirée`);
      if (cam.smooth) renderCands(topK(cam.smooth, 6));
    }
  }

  // Boutons « Scanner » ajoutés aux barres d'outils
  function addButtons() {
    const fmTools = document.querySelector('.shelf.fm .tools');
    const vinTools = document.querySelector('.shelf.vin .tools');
    if (fmTools && !$('scanCardBtn')) {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.id = 'scanCardBtn'; b.className = 'primary scan-btn'; b.textContent = '📷 Scanner des cartes';
      b.addEventListener('click', () => open('card'));
      fmTools.prepend(b);
    }
    if (vinTools && !$('scanVinBtn')) {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.id = 'scanVinBtn'; b.className = 'primary scan-btn'; b.textContent = '📷 Scanner un code-barres';
      b.addEventListener('click', () => open('vinyl'));
      vinTools.prepend(b);
    }
  }
  addButtons();
  // Prépare la reconnaissance en arrière-plan pour que le premier scan soit immédiat
  setTimeout(() => prepareRefs(), 5000);

  window.CoffreScan = {
    openCards: () => open('card'),
    openBarcode: () => open('vinyl'),
    scanQr() { return new Promise(resolve => { if (qrResolve) qrResolve(''); qrResolve = resolve; open('qr'); }); },
    isOpen: () => !!$('scanView') && !$('scanView').hidden,
    close,
    // pour les tests
    _describeCard: describeCard, _scoreRect: scoreRect, _topK: topK, _prepareRefs: prepareRefs, _ref: REF
  };
})();

// Prépare le dossier www/ de l'appli Android à partir de l'interface du PC.
// L'interface (app.js, styles.css, fm-data.js) est partagée : une modif sur PC arrive aussi sur le téléphone.
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const M = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const ROOT = path.join(M, '..');
const R = path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'renderer');
const WWW = path.join(M, 'www');

fs.rmSync(WWW, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(WWW, 'cards'), { recursive: true });

const copy = (from, to) => fs.copyFileSync(from, path.join(WWW, to));
copy(path.join(R, 'app.js'), 'app.js');
copy(path.join(R, 'styles.css'), 'styles.css');
copy(path.join(R, 'fm-data.js'), 'fm-data.js');
copy(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'shared', 'coffre-core.js'), 'coffre-core.js');
copy(path.join(M, 'node_modules', '@zxing', 'library', 'umd', 'index.min.js'), 'zxing.js');
for (const f of fs.readdirSync(path.join(M, 'src'))) copy(path.join(M, 'src', f), f);

const cards = path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'assets', 'cards');
let n = 0;
for (const f of fs.readdirSync(cards)) if (f.endsWith('.webp')) { fs.copyFileSync(path.join(cards, f), path.join(WWW, 'cards', f)); n++; }

// Version de l'appli = version du PC (package.json à la racine)
const version = process.env.APP_VERSION || require(path.join(ROOT, 'package.json')).version;

let html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(R, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
html = html
  .replace(/<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy"[^>]*>/,
    `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob: https: http:; media-src 'self' blob: mediastream:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self'; connect-src 'self' https: http:">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#1b1a1f">`)
  .replace('<link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css">', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css">\n  <link rel="stylesheet" href="mobile.css">')
  .replace('<script src="fm-data.js"></script>',
    `<script src="version.js"></script>
  <script src="fm-data.js"></script>
  <script src="coffre-core.js"></script>
  <script src="zxing.js"></script>
  <script src="mobile.js"></script>`)
  .replace('<script src="app.js"></script>', '<script src="app.js"></script>\n  <script src="scan.js"></script>');
if (!html.includes('mobile.js') || !html.includes('scan.js')) throw new Error("index.html n'a pas pu être adapté");
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(WWW, 'index.html'), html);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(WWW, 'version.js'), `window.COFFRE_VERSION = ${JSON.stringify(version)};\n`);
console.log(`www prêt : ${n} images de cartes, version ${version}`);
mobile/scripts/patch-android.js nouveau
// Ajuste le projet Android généré par Capacitor : appareil photo, réseau local, version, icône.
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const M = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const A = path.join(M, 'android', 'app');
const version = process.env.APP_VERSION || require(path.join(M, '..', 'package.json')).version;
const [maj, min, pat] = version.split('.').map(n => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
const versionCode = maj * 10000 + min * 100 + pat;

// 1. Autorisations : appareil photo + connexion au PC en HTTP sur le Wi-Fi
const mf = path.join(A, 'src', 'main', 'AndroidManifest.xml');
let xml = fs.readFileSync(mf, 'utf8');
if (!xml.includes('android.permission.CAMERA')) {
  xml = xml.replace('<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />',
    '<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />\n    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.CAMERA" />\n    <uses-feature android:name="android.hardware.camera" android:required="false" />');
}
if (!xml.includes('usesCleartextTraffic')) xml = xml.replace('<application', '<application\n        android:usesCleartextTraffic="true"');
if (!xml.includes('android.permission.CAMERA') || !xml.includes('usesCleartextTraffic')) throw new Error('AndroidManifest.xml : modification impossible');
fs.writeFileSync(mf, xml);

// 2. Numéro de version (le même que l'appli PC)
const bg = path.join(A, 'build.gradle');
let gradle = fs.readFileSync(bg, 'utf8');
gradle = gradle.replace(/versionCode \d+/, `versionCode ${versionCode}`).replace(/versionName "[^"]*"/, `versionName "${version}"`);
if (!gradle.includes(`versionName "${version}"`)) throw new Error('build.gradle : version introuvable');
fs.writeFileSync(bg, gradle);

// 3. Icône et écran de démarrage
const copyDir = (from, to) => {
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(from, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const s = path.join(from, e.name), d = path.join(to, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) { fs.mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true }); copyDir(s, d); } else fs.copyFileSync(s, d);
  }
};
copyDir(path.join(M, 'res-android'), path.join(A, 'src', 'main', 'res'));

console.log(`Projet Android prêt : version ${version} (code ${versionCode})`);

src/main.js nouveau — Processus principal : stockage, recherche MusicBrainz/Discogs, pochettes, sauvegardes, mises à jour
// Coffre — processus principal Electron
// Gère la fenêtre, le stockage local, la recherche de vinyles (MusicBrainz / Discogs),
// les pochettes et les mises à jour automatiques via GitHub Releases.

const { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain, dialog, shell } = require('electron');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { pathToFileURL } = require('url');
const crypto = require('crypto');

let autoUpdater = null;
try { autoUpdater = require('electron-updater').autoUpdater; } catch (e) { autoUpdater = null; }

const USER_AGENT = `Coffre/${app.getVersion()} ( https://github.com/BlancheLaGoat/Coffre )`;

// ---------- Chemins ----------
const DATA_DIR = app.getPath('userData');            // %AppData%\Coffre
const DATA_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'coffre.json');
const PREV_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'coffre.precedent.json');
const COVERS_DIR = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'pochettes');

function ensureDirs() {
  fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
  fs.mkdirSync(COVERS_DIR, { recursive: true });
}

function defaultData() {
  return { version: 1, fm: {}, vinyls: {}, settings: { source: 'musicbrainz', discogsToken: '', layout: 'gal' } };
}

function loadData() {
  ensureDirs();
  for (const f of [DATA_FILE, PREV_FILE]) {
    try {
      if (fs.existsSync(f)) {
        const d = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'));
        const base = defaultData();
        return { ...base, ...d, settings: { ...base.settings, ...(d.settings || {}) } };
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fichier abîmé : on essaie le précédent */ }
  }
  return defaultData();
}

function saveData(data) {
  ensureDirs();
  const tmp = DATA_FILE + '.tmp';
  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'utf8');
  if (fs.existsSync(DATA_FILE)) fs.copyFileSync(DATA_FILE, PREV_FILE);
  fs.renameSync(tmp, DATA_FILE);
  return true;
}

// ---------- Réseau ----------
async function getJson(url, headers = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'User-Agent': USER_AGENT, Accept: 'application/json', ...headers } });
  if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) throw new Error('Clé Discogs refusée. Vérifie ton token dans les réglages.');
  if (res.status === 429 || res.status === 503) throw new Error('Le site limite les recherches. Attends quelques secondes et réessaie.');
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Le serveur a répondu ${res.status}.`);
  return res.json();
}

// MusicBrainz : gratuit, sans clé. Pochettes via Cover Art Archive.
function mbArtist(credit) {
  return (credit || []).map(c => (c.name || c.artist?.name || '') + (c.joinphrase || '')).join('').trim();
}

async function searchMusicBrainz(q, page) {
  const t = q.replace(/[\\+\-!(){}\[\]^"~*?:/]|&&|\|\|/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!t) return { results: [], hasMore: false };
  const query = `(release:(${t}) OR artist:(${t}) OR label:(${t}) OR catno:(${t})) AND format:vinyl`;
  const limit = 30;
  const url = `https://musicbrainz.org/ws/2/release/?query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&fmt=json&limit=${limit}&offset=${(page - 1) * limit}`;
  const d = await getJson(url);
  const results = (d.releases || []).map(r => {
    const li = (r['label-info'] || [])[0] || {};
    const formats = (r.media || []).map(m => m.format).filter(Boolean);
    const discs = (r.media || []).length;
    return {
      source: 'musicbrainz',
      id: r.id,
      artist: mbArtist(r['artist-credit']),
      title: r.title,
      year: (r.date || '').slice(0, 4),
      country: r.country || '',
      label: li.label?.name || '',
      catno: li['catalog-number'] || '',
      format: discs > 1 ? `${discs}× ${formats[0] || 'Vinyl'}` : (formats[0] || 'Vinyl'),
      thumb: `https://coverartarchive.org/release/${r.id}/front-250`,
      cover: `https://coverartarchive.org/release/${r.id}/front-500`
    };
  });
  return { results, hasMore: (d.count || 0) > page * limit };
}

function msToTime(ms) {
  if (!ms) return '';
  const s = Math.round(ms / 1000);
  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

async function detailsMusicBrainz(id) {
  const r = await getJson(`https://musicbrainz.org/ws/2/release/${id}?inc=recordings+labels+artist-credits+release-groups&fmt=json`);
  const tracklist = [];
  (r.media || []).forEach((m, i) => {
    (m.tracks || []).forEach(t => tracklist.push({ pos: (r.media.length > 1 ? `${i + 1}-` : '') + (t.number || ''), title: t.title, duration: msToTime(t.length) }));
  });
  const li = (r['label-info'] || [])[0] || {};
  return {
    tracklist,
    genres: [],
    label: li.label?.name || '',
    catno: li['catalog-number'] || '',
    country: r.country || '',
    year: (r.date || '').slice(0, 4),
    cover: `https://coverartarchive.org/release/${id}/front-500`,
    url: `https://musicbrainz.org/release/${id}`
  };
}

// Discogs : nécessite un token personnel gratuit (discogs.com/settings/developers).
async function searchDiscogs(q, page, token) {
  if (!token) throw new Error('Ajoute ton token Discogs dans les réglages pour chercher sur Discogs.');
  const url = `https://api.discogs.com/database/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&type=release&format=Vinyl&per_page=30&page=${page}`;
  const d = await getJson(url, { Authorization: `Discogs token=${token}` });
  const results = (d.results || []).map(r => {
    const [artist, ...rest] = (r.title || '').split(' - ');
    return {
      source: 'discogs',
      id: String(r.id),
      artist: rest.length ? artist : '',
      title: rest.length ? rest.join(' - ') : r.title,
      year: r.year ? String(r.year) : '',
      country: r.country || '',
      label: (r.label || [])[0] || '',
      catno: r.catno || '',
      format: (r.format || []).slice(0, 3).join(', '),
      thumb: r.thumb || r.cover_image || '',
      cover: r.cover_image || r.thumb || ''
    };
  });
  return { results, hasMore: (d.pagination?.pages || 1) > page };
}

async function detailsDiscogs(id, token) {
  const d = await getJson(`https://api.discogs.com/releases/${id}`, token ? { Authorization: `Discogs token=${token}` } : {});
  const primary = (d.images || []).find(i => i.type === 'primary') || (d.images || [])[0];
  const fmt = (d.formats || [])[0];
  return {
    tracklist: (d.tracklist || []).filter(t => t.type_ !== 'heading').map(t => ({ pos: t.position, title: t.title, duration: t.duration })),
    genres: [...(d.genres || []), ...(d.styles || [])],
    label: d.labels?.[0]?.name || '',
    catno: d.labels?.[0]?.catno || '',
    country: d.country || '',
    year: d.year ? String(d.year) : '',
    format: fmt ? [fmt.qty && fmt.qty !== '1' ? `${fmt.qty}×` : '', fmt.name, ...(fmt.descriptions || [])].filter(Boolean).join(' ') : '',
    cover: primary?.uri || '',
    url: d.uri || `https://www.discogs.com/release/${id}`
  };
}

// ---------- Pochettes ----------
function coverName(ext) {
  return `${Date.now()}-${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}${ext}`;
}

async function downloadCover(url) {
  if (!url) return '';
  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'User-Agent': USER_AGENT } });
  if (!res.ok) return '';
  const type = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  const ext = type.includes('png') ? '.png' : type.includes('webp') ? '.webp' : '.jpg';
  const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  ensureDirs();
  const name = coverName(ext);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(COVERS_DIR, name), buf);
  return name;
}

function safeCoverPath(name) {
  const base = path.basename(String(name || ''));
  return base ? path.join(COVERS_DIR, base) : '';
}

// ---------- Fenêtre ----------
let win = null;

function createWindow() {
  win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1440,
    height: 900,
    minWidth: 900,
    minHeight: 600,
    backgroundColor: '#14121b',
    title: 'Coffre',
    icon: path.join(__dirname, 'assets', 'icon.png'),
    autoHideMenuBar: true,
    webPreferences: {
      preload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false,
      sandbox: true
    }
  });
  win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, 'renderer', 'index.html'));

  // Les liens externes (Discogs, Cardmarket…) s'ouvrent dans le navigateur.
  win.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) shell.openExternal(url);
    return { action: 'deny' };
  });
  win.webContents.on('will-navigate', (e, url) => {
    if (!url.startsWith('file:')) { e.preventDefault(); if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) shell.openExternal(url); }
  });
}

function sendStatus(status, extra = {}) {
  if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.webContents.send('update:status', { status, ...extra });
}

function setupUpdater() {
  if (!autoUpdater || !app.isPackaged) return;
  autoUpdater.autoDownload = true;
  autoUpdater.autoInstallOnAppQuit = true;
  autoUpdater.on('checking-for-update', () => sendStatus('checking'));
  autoUpdater.on('update-available', i => sendStatus('available', { version: i.version }));
  autoUpdater.on('update-not-available', () => sendStatus('none'));
  autoUpdater.on('download-progress', p => sendStatus('downloading', { percent: Math.round(p.percent) }));
  autoUpdater.on('update-downloaded', i => sendStatus('ready', { version: i.version }));
  autoUpdater.on('error', err => sendStatus('error', { message: String(err?.message || err) }));
  autoUpdater.checkForUpdates().catch(() => {});
  setInterval(() => autoUpdater.checkForUpdates().catch(() => {}), 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
}

// ---------- IPC ----------
ipcMain.handle('app:info', () => ({
  version: app.getVersion(),
  packaged: app.isPackaged,
  coversUrl: pathToFileURL(COVERS_DIR + path.sep).href,
  dataDir: DATA_DIR
}));

ipcMain.handle('data:load', () => loadData());
ipcMain.handle('data:save', (_e, data) => saveData(data));

ipcMain.handle('vinyl:search', async (_e, { q, source, page = 1, token }) => {
  return source === 'discogs' ? searchDiscogs(q, page, token) : searchMusicBrainz(q, page);
});

ipcMain.handle('vinyl:details', async (_e, { source, id, token }) => {
  return source === 'discogs' ? detailsDiscogs(id, token) : detailsMusicBrainz(id);
});

ipcMain.handle('cover:download', async (_e, url) => {
  try { return await downloadCover(url); } catch (e) { return ''; }
});

ipcMain.handle('cover:pick', async () => {
  const r = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
    title: 'Choisir une pochette',
    properties: ['openFile'],
    filters: [{ name: 'Images', extensions: ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'webp'] }]
  });
  if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths[0]) return '';
  const src = r.filePaths[0];
  ensureDirs();
  const name = coverName(path.extname(src).toLowerCase() || '.jpg');
  fs.copyFileSync(src, path.join(COVERS_DIR, name));
  return name;
});

ipcMain.handle('cover:delete', (_e, name) => {
  const p = safeCoverPath(name);
  try { if (p && fs.existsSync(p)) fs.unlinkSync(p); } catch (e) { /* ignoré */ }
  return true;
});

// Sauvegarde complète : données + pochettes, dans un seul fichier .coffre (JSON).
ipcMain.handle('backup:export', async (_e, data) => {
  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const r = await dialog.showSaveDialog(win, {
    title: 'Exporter une sauvegarde',
    defaultPath: `Coffre-sauvegarde-${stamp}.coffre`,
    filters: [{ name: 'Sauvegarde Coffre', extensions: ['coffre'] }]
  });
  if (r.canceled || !r.filePath) return { ok: false };
  const covers = {};
  for (const v of Object.values(data.vinyls || {})) {
    const p = safeCoverPath(v.cover);
    if (p && fs.existsSync(p)) covers[path.basename(p)] = fs.readFileSync(p).toString('base64');
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(r.filePath, JSON.stringify({ format: 'coffre-backup', version: 1, data, covers }));
  return { ok: true, path: r.filePath };
});

ipcMain.handle('backup:import', async () => {
  const r = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
    title: 'Importer une sauvegarde',
    properties: ['openFile'],
    filters: [{ name: 'Sauvegarde Coffre', extensions: ['coffre', 'json'] }]
  });
  if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths[0]) return { ok: false };
  const b = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(r.filePaths[0], 'utf8'));
  const data = b.format === 'coffre-backup' ? b.data : b;
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object' || (!data.fm && !data.vinyls)) throw new Error("Ce fichier n'est pas une sauvegarde Coffre.");
  ensureDirs();
  for (const [name, b64] of Object.entries(b.covers || {})) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(COVERS_DIR, path.basename(name)), Buffer.from(b64, 'base64'));
  }
  const merged = { ...defaultData(), ...data, settings: { ...defaultData().settings, ...(data.settings || {}) } };
  saveData(merged);
  return { ok: true, data: merged };
});

ipcMain.handle('open:external', (_e, url) => { if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) shell.openExternal(url); });
ipcMain.handle('open:dataDir', () => shell.openPath(DATA_DIR));

ipcMain.handle('update:check', async () => {
  if (!autoUpdater || !app.isPackaged) return { status: 'dev' };
  try { await autoUpdater.checkForUpdates(); return { status: 'checking' }; }
  catch (e) { return { status: 'error', message: String(e?.message || e) }; }
});
ipcMain.handle('update:install', () => { if (autoUpdater) autoUpdater.quitAndInstall(); });

// ---------- Démarrage ----------
if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  app.on('second-instance', () => { if (win) { if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore(); win.focus(); } });
  app.whenReady().then(() => {
    ensureDirs();
    createWindow();
    setupUpdater();
    app.on('activate', () => { if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) createWindow(); });
  });
  app.on('window-all-closed', () => { if (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit(); });
}
src/preload.js nouveau — Pont sécurisé entre l’interface et le processus principal
// Pont sécurisé entre l'interface et le processus principal.
const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('coffre', {
  info: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('app:info'),
  load: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('data:load'),
  save: data => ipcRenderer.invoke('data:save', data),
  search: args => ipcRenderer.invoke('vinyl:search', args),
  details: args => ipcRenderer.invoke('vinyl:details', args),
  downloadCover: url => ipcRenderer.invoke('cover:download', url),
  pickCover: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('cover:pick'),
  deleteCover: name => ipcRenderer.invoke('cover:delete', name),
  exportBackup: data => ipcRenderer.invoke('backup:export', data),
  importBackup: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('backup:import'),
  openExternal: url => ipcRenderer.invoke('open:external', url),
  openDataDir: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('open:dataDir'),
  checkUpdate: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('update:check'),
  installUpdate: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('update:install'),
  onUpdate: cb => ipcRenderer.on('update:status', (_e, s) => cb(s))
});
